The pattern is that set one is consistently too light. He has already been told this once about the leg press; the general version is worth saying once, clearly.

Nothing is waiting on Daniel. Zero workout comments on all eight recent sessions. Iris to-dos read "No action needed". The Aug 31 belt question was answered the same day.

🎯 Scan-Day Focus: Frame The Window First, Then Settle Build Versus Burn
1. Have an answer ready for the sentence she already said. On Sep 17 she said "if numbers don't show it, I don't know what we're going to do." She has trained 12 of 13, pushed her steps to 9,200 and adopted every food change. Decide before you walk in what you say if the numbers are flat, because she will ask. The honest answer is that everything that was supposed to change only changed on Sep 2 and Sep 17.
2. Open on the split squat and the steps. 50 for three sets on Sep 9 to 70 for 8, 10 and 10 on Sep 18. Steps from a 6,689 weekly average to 9,200 a day. Both are hers, both are recent, both are unambiguous.
3. Frame the window before the numbers. 26 days, and the Gut Fire Protocol plus the harder-sets instruction both started at the very top of it. The Aug 3 to Sep 2 result is last block's, not this one's. Say this first or a good month gets read as a second failure.
4. Settle build versus burn out loud. Record says lean mass gain off ALMI at the 42nd percentile against a 60 to 70 target. Her focus and the Sep 17 plan say burn. The answer is hold the muscle while the fat comes off, and she needs to hear it as one plan rather than two.
5. Name the flat sets with her own numbers. Hammer curl 20 for 7, 7, 7 and heels-elevated goblet squat 35 for 10, 10, 10 against the hip thrust at 125 for 10, 8, 7, 7. She will recognize both instantly, and the second one proves she can already do it.
6. Fix the bench instruction properly. The 30s do not exist at her sister's. Give her the rest-pause version as the default there and the 30s wherever she has them, or the instruction quietly fails for a third week.
7. Leave out: bone density, any calorie target, the ALMI kg/m² value (percentile only), and the Gold Standard numbers, which are inflated by her current body weight and would read as a wall.
8. No renewal question and no close. Routine package checkpoint inside a 6 Month Core term running to Feb 3, 2027. Draft 4 skipped by the gate and there is no night-before Slack post for this member (scan_next.type != "second"). And check the name on any printout before you hand it to her.
